  var externalAbort=null;
  if(controller&&externalSignal){if(externalSignal.aborted){try{controller.abort()}catch(_){ }}else{externalAbort=function(){try{controller.abort()}catch(_){ }};try{externalSignal.addEventListener('abort',externalAbort,{once:true})}catch(_){ }}}

   if(timer)native.clearTimeout(timer);if(externalAbort&&externalSignal)try{externalSignal.removeEventListener('abort',externalAbort)}catch(_){ }

function isSessionError(error){return ['SESSION_INVALID','SESSION_REVOKED','AUTH_REQUIRED'].indexOf(error&&error.code)>=0}
async function verifyCurrentSession(){
 if(!text(runtime.authToken)){const e=new Error('Die Anmeldung lieferte keinen gültigen Sitzungsschlüssel.');e.code='AUTH_TOKEN_MISSING';throw e}
 const d=await authCall('session',{});
 if(!d||d.ok===false){const e=new Error(d&&d.message||'Die Sitzung konnte nicht bestätigt werden.');e.code=d&&d.code||'SESSION_CHECK_FAILED';throw e}
 runtime.user=d.user||runtime.user;
 runtime.users=d.user?[d.user]:runtime.users;
 writeTabSession();
 return d
}
async function renewSessionOnce(){
 const pending=runtime.loginRetryCredentials;
 if(!pending||runtime.sessionRefreshAttempted)return false;
 runtime.sessionRefreshAttempted=true;
 status('Die Sitzung wird einmalig erneuert …','');
 const fresh=await authCall('login',{username:pending.username,password:pending.password,deviceId:pending.deviceId,recoveryRequested:false},'');
 if(!fresh||!text(fresh.token)||fresh.mustChange===true)return false;
 runtime.authToken=text(fresh.token);runtime.user=fresh.user||runtime.user;runtime.users=fresh.user?[fresh.user]:runtime.users;
 await verifyCurrentSession();
 return true
}
async function verifySessionForLoad(){
 var attempts=runtime.sessionRestored?3:1,lastError=null;
 for(var i=0;i<attempts;i++){
  try{return await verifyCurrentSession()}catch(error){lastError=error;if(!isSessionError(error)||i===attempts-1)throw error;await new Promise(function(resolve){native.setTimeout(resolve,350*(i+1))})}
 }
 throw lastError
}

}

function isSessionError(error){return ['SESSION_INVALID','SESSION_REVOKED','AUTH_REQUIRED'].indexOf(error&&error.code)>=0}
async function verifyCurrentSession(){
 if(!text(runtime.authToken)){const e=new Error('Die Anmeldung lieferte keinen gültigen Sitzungsschlüssel.');e.code='AUTH_TOKEN_MISSING';throw e}
 const d=await authCall('session',{});
 if(!d||d.ok===false){const e=new Error(d&&d.message||'Die Sitzung konnte nicht bestätigt werden.');e.code=d&&d.code||'SESSION_CHECK_FAILED';throw e}
 runtime.user=d.user||runtime.user;
 runtime.users=d.user?[d.user]:runtime.users;
 writeTabSession();
 return d
}
async function renewSessionOnce(){
 const pending=runtime.loginRetryCredentials;
 if(!pending||runtime.sessionRefreshAttempted)return false;
 runtime.sessionRefreshAttempted=true;
 status('Die Sitzung wird einmalig erneuert …','');
 const fresh=await authCall('login',{username:pending.username,password:pending.password,deviceId:pending.deviceId,recoveryRequested:false},'');
 if(!fresh||!text(fresh.token)||fresh.mustChange===true)return false;
 runtime.authToken=text(fresh.token);runtime.user=fresh.user||runtime.user;runtime.users=fresh.user?[fresh.user]:runtime.users;
 await verifyCurrentSession();
 return true
}
async function verifySessionForLoad(){
 var attempts=runtime.sessionRestored?3:1,lastError=null;
 for(var i=0;i<attempts;i++){
  try{return await verifyCurrentSession()}catch(error){lastError=error;if(!isSessionError(error)||i===attempts-1)throw error;await new Promise(function(resolve){native.setTimeout(resolve,350*(i+1))})}
 }
 throw lastError
}

function isTransientLoginLoadError(error){var code=String(error&&error.code||''),statusCode=Number(error&&error.status||0);return ['API_UNREACHABLE','API_TIMEOUT','STATE_API_UNREACHABLE','INVALID_SERVER_JSON','HTTP_408','HTTP_425','HTTP_429','HTTP_500','HTTP_502','HTTP_503','HTTP_504'].indexOf(code)>=0||[408,425,429,500,502,503,504].indexOf(statusCode)>=0}
async function loadStateAfterLogin(){
 if(isTestServiceOrigin()){
  try{return await loadState({timeoutMs:14000,maxAttempts:1})}
  catch(firstError){
   if(!isTransientLoginLoadError(firstError))throw firstError;
   status('TESTSERVICE-Anmeldung bestätigt · Azure-Teamdaten antworten verzögert. Ein kontrollierter Wiederholungsversuch läuft …','');
   progress(12,'Azure-Teamdaten antworten verzögert · einmaliger Wiederholungsversuch …');
   await new Promise(function(resolve){native.setTimeout(resolve,1200)});
   return loadState({timeoutMs:24000,maxAttempts:1})
  }
 }
 try{return await loadState({timeoutMs:25000,maxAttempts:1})}catch(firstError){if(isSessionError(firstError)&&await renewSessionOnce())return loadState({timeoutMs:25000,maxAttempts:1});throw firstError}
}

function load(){request('pickup-status?token='+encodeURIComponent(token),{method:'GET'}).then(show).catch(function(e){status(e.status===404?'Dieser QR-Code ist nicht registriert oder nicht mehr gültig.':'Sendungsdaten konnten nicht geladen werden: '+q(e.message||e),'bad')})}
function verifySignatureImage(){return fetch('/api/pickup-pod?token='+encodeURIComponent(token)+'&signature=1&verify='+Date.now(),{method:'GET',credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',headers:{Accept:'image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp'}}).then(function(r){var ct=String(r.headers.get('content-type')||'').toLowerCase();if(!r.ok)throw new Error('HTTP '+r.status);if(!/^image\/(?:jpeg|png|webp)/.test(ct))throw new Error('Der Server liefert keine digitale Unterschrift.');return r.blob()}).then(function(b){if(!b||b.size<100)throw new Error('Die gespeicherte digitale Unterschrift ist leer.');return true})}
function verify(response){var attempts=0,maxAttempts=15;function poll(){attempts++;return request('pickup-status?token='+encodeURIComponent(token)+'&verify='+Date.now(),{method:'GET',timeout:12000}).then(function(data){var merged=Object.assign({},response||{},data||{});if(!confirmed(merged)){if(attempts<maxAttempts)return new Promise(function(resolve){setTimeout(resolve,2000)}).then(poll);var e=new Error('Der Server hat die Abholung noch nicht als bestätigt zurückgemeldet. Bitte nicht erneut bestätigen.');e.code='CONFIRM_NOT_VISIBLE';throw e}var inline=q(valueDeep(merged,['driverSignature','pickupDriverSignature','signatureDataUrl','pickupSignature','signature','qrPickupSignature'])),url=q(valueDeep(merged,['driverSignatureUrl','pickupDriverSignatureUrl','pickupSignatureUrl','signatureUrl']));if(/^data:image\/(?:jpeg|png|webp);base64,/i.test(inline)||url)return merged;return verifySignatureImage().then(function(){merged.signatureAvailable=true;merged.driverSignatureUrl='/api/pickup-pod?token='+encodeURIComponent(token)+'&signature=1';return merged})}).catch(function(err){if((err.timeout||err.code==='REQUEST_TIMEOUT')&&attempts<maxAttempts)return new Promise(function(resolve){setTimeout(resolve,2000)}).then(poll);throw err})}return poll()}

function stowLdm(r){var v=num(r.ldm);if(v>0||q(r.ldm)!=='')return v;var name=low(r.type||r.packaging||r.verpackung),master=PACK.find(function(p){return low(p.name)===name});if(master&&master.ldm!==undefined&&master.ldm!==null)return num(master.ldm);var l=num(r.l),w=num(r.w);return l>0&&w>0?round(l*w/24000):0}
function stowDimensions(r){var name=low(r.type||r.packaging||r.verpackung),master=PACK.find(function(p){return low(p.name)===name}),l=num(r.l||r.length)||(master&&num(master.l))||0,w=num(r.w||r.width)||(master&&num(master.w))||0,h=num(r.h||r.height)||(master&&num(master.h))||0,estimated=false;if(!(l>0&&w>0)){var area=Math.max(.01,stowLdm(r))*24000;l=120;w=Math.max(20,Math.min(240,area/l));estimated=true}return{l:Math.round(l),w:Math.round(w),h:Math.round(h),estimated:estimated}}
function stowOrientationOptions(r){

[{depth:d.l,cross:d.w,rotated:false},{depth:d.w,cross:d.l,rotated:true}].forEach(function(x){
 if(!(x.depth>0&&x.cross>0))return;
 x.maxAcross=Math.max(1,Math.min(6,Math.floor(245/Math.max(1,x.cross))||1));
 x.dim=d;out.push(x)
});
if(!out.length)out.push({depth:Math.max(20,d.l||120),cross:Math.min(245,Math.max(20,d.w||80)),rotated:false,maxAcross:1,dim:d});
return out
}

function chooseStowLayout(options,remainingFloor,vehicleWidth){var best=null,width=Math.max(120,num(vehicleWidth)||245);arr(options).forEach(function(o){if(!(num(o.cross)>0&&num(o.cross)<=width&&num(o.depth)>0))return;var across=Math.max(1,Math.floor(width/num(o.cross))||1),take=Math.max(1,Math.min(across,remainingFloor)),used=take*num(o.cross),gap=Math.max(0,width-used),fill=used/width,exact=gap<=8?1:0,close=gap<=20?1:0,score=(exact*100000)+(close*50000)+(fill*10000)+(take*800)-(num(o.depth)*10)-gap+(!o.rotated?5:0);if(!best||score>best.score)best={depth:num(o.depth),cross:num(o.cross),rotated:!!o.rotated,maxAcross:across,dim:o.dim||{},take:take,used:used,gap:gap,score:score}});if(!best){var fallback=arr(options)[0]||{depth:120,cross:80,rotated:false,dim:{}};best={depth:num(fallback.depth)||120,cross:Math.min(width,Math.max(20,num(fallback.cross)||80)),rotated:!!fallback.rotated,maxAcross:1,dim:fallback.dim||{},take:1,used:Math.min(width,Math.max(20,num(fallback.cross)||80)),gap:Math.max(0,width-Math.min(width,Math.max(20,num(fallback.cross)||80))),score:0}}
return best}
function buildStowPlan(rows,vehicle){
vehicle=vehicle||currentStowVehicle();
var TRAILER_LENGTH=Math.max(300,num(vehicle.lengthCm)||1360),TRAILER_WIDTH=Math.max(120,num(vehicle.widthCm)||245),groups=[],totalWeight=0,totalUnits=0,totalFloorUnits=0;
rows.forEach(function(r,rowIndex){
 var physicalCount=Math.max(0,Math.round(num(r.count))),stackFactor=stowStackFactor(r),floorCount=Math.ceil(physicalCount/stackFactor),options=stowOrientationOptions(r),dims=stowDimensions(r),weightTotal=Math.max(0,num(r.weight)),weightEachPhysical=physicalCount?weightTotal/physicalCount:0,type=q(r.type)||'Ladung',high=num(dims&&dims.h)>=170,pallet=/palette|paletten|pallet/i.test(type);
 if(!physicalCount)return;
 groups.push({rowIndex:rowIndex+1,type:type,physicalCount:physicalCount,remainingPhysical:physicalCount,floorCount:floorCount,remainingFloor:floorCount,stackFactor:stackFactor,weightEachPhysical:weightEachPhysical,weightTotal:weightTotal,options:options,dims:dims,pallet:pallet,high:high,priority:0});
 totalWeight+=weightTotal;totalUnits+=physicalCount;totalFloorUnits+=floorCount
});
groups.sort(function(a,b){var ah=num(a.dims&&a.dims.h),bh=num(b.dims&&b.dims.h),aPal=a.pallet?1:0,bPal=b.pallet?1:0;if(ah!==bh)return bh-ah;if(aPal!==bPal)return bPal-aPal;if(a.weightEachPhysical!==b.weightEachPhysical)return b.weightEachPhysical-a.weightEachPhysical;return((num(b.dims&&b.dims.l)*num(b.dims&&b.dims.w))-(num(a.dims&&a.dims.l)*num(a.dims&&a.dims.w)))||(a.rowIndex-b.rowIndex)});
groups.forEach(function(g,index){g.priority=Math.max(1,groups.length-index)});
function remainingAny(){return groups.some(function(g){return g.remainingFloor>0})}
function candidateScore(group,opt,remainingWidth,isAnchor){var postGap=Math.max(0,remainingWidth-num(opt.cross)),exact=postGap<=8?250000:(postGap<=20?120000:0),fill=num(opt.cross)*120,rank=num(group.priority||1)*6000,palletBonus=group.pallet?900:0,highBonus=group.high?2200:0,anchorBonus=isAnchor?4500:0,rotatePenalty=opt.rotated?80:0;return exact+fill+rank+palletBonus+highBonus+anchorBonus-rotatePenalty-postGap}
function simulateBand(anchor,opt){var depth=num(opt.depth),remainingWidth=Math.max(0,TRAILER_WIDTH-num(opt.cross)),temp=groups.map(function(g){return g.remainingFloor}),units=[{group:anchor,opt:opt}],guard=0;
 temp[groups.indexOf(anchor)]--;
 while(remainingWidth>=20&&guard<32){guard++;var best=null;groups.forEach(function(g,gi){if(temp[gi]<=0)return;arr(g.options).forEach(function(option){if(!(num(option.depth)>0&&num(option.depth)<=depth))return;if(!(num(option.cross)>0&&num(option.cross)<=remainingWidth))return;var depthWaste=(depth-num(option.depth))*num(option.cross),score=candidateScore(g,option,remainingWidth,false)-depthWaste*6;if(!best||score>best.score)best={group:g,opt:option,gi:gi,score:score}})});if(!best)break;temp[best.gi]--;remainingWidth=Math.max(0,remainingWidth-num(best.opt.cross));units.push({group:best.group,opt:best.opt})}
 function estimateFuture(){var total=0;groups.forEach(function(g,gi){var left=Math.max(0,Number(temp[gi]||0));if(!left)return;var best=Infinity;arr(g.options).forEach(function(o){var cross=num(o.cross),d=num(o.depth);if(!(cross>0&&cross<=TRAILER_WIDTH&&d>0))return;var across=Math.max(1,Math.floor(TRAILER_WIDTH/cross)),rowsNeeded=Math.ceil(left/across),lengthNeeded=rowsNeeded*d;if(lengthNeeded<best)best=lengthNeeded});if(isFinite(best))total+=best});return total}
 var used=TRAILER_WIDTH-remainingWidth,gap=Math.max(0,remainingWidth),futureCm=estimateFuture(),projectedCm=depth+futureCm,score=-(projectedCm*100000)+(used*1200)+(gap<=8?180000:(gap<=20?80000:0))+(units.length*500)+(anchor.high?6000:0)+(anchor.pallet?2500:0)-gap*50;
 return{anchor:anchor,opt:opt,depth:depth,units:units,used:used,gap:gap,score:score,futureCm:futureCm,projectedCm:projectedCm}
}
